### Speak with Robin

> *Ratite? Who's that? Is that like a username? Some big-time influencer with a bunch of viral videos? What do they want with you? Talk it over with Robin and Siobhan. "What's it like being completely clueless that you might be a cosmic superstar?" "Thanks for the question. Just logged on and found out someone's put a 200,000-credit bounty on me just to get a face-to-face."*

[Recap] Robin, invited to perform at the opening ceremony of Starflux, had just wrapped up rehearsals and set aside a few tickets for you. Her other reason for making the trip was to reclaim the Lost Echo of Gopher Wood, which had turned up on the private auction list of Ratite, the internet celebrity in Astropolis. In the meantime, Ratite had sent you an invitation to meet.

**Robin:** I'm sorry, I planned on meeting somewhere more official, but my schedule's been packed. So, we can only meet here for now.

> **知更鸟：**不好意思呀，本来想约在更正式的地方，但通告密集，只能临时在这里见面了。

**Robin:** If I can help with anything, let me know right away.

[Recap] On the eve of the Anti-Destruction Alliance summit, Antares, The Family's representative, proposed sending Robin to perform in Astropolis, using the concert as a means to expand the Harmony's influence. Robin sensed that a tuning had been placed on her, but played along on the surface.

**"Antares":** Since when has the Harmony's dance ever needed the Preservation's guidance?

> **「心宿二」：**「同谐」的舞步，何时需要「存护」指引？

**Robin:** Does The Family not wish to join the Anti-Destruction Alliance?

> **知更鸟：**「家族」不希望加入「反毁灭同盟」？

**"Antares":** Precisely the opposite. This is a rare chance.

> **「心宿二」：**恰恰相反，这是个千载难逢的机会。

**"Antares":** Trailblaze, Preservation, The Hunt, Elation... Even Destruction. Each and every one is an isolated instrument.

> **「心宿二」：**开拓、存护、巡猎、欢愉…甚至是毁灭，不过是各自孤立的器乐。

**"Antares":** The Family is a far better leader than the hypocritical IPC, and better at commanding than the Nameless who blindly forge ahead.

> **「心宿二」：**「家族」比伪善的公司更擅长引导，比闷头前进的无名客更擅长指挥。

**"Antares":** So, turn them into notes in a rhapsody, and pacify them with harmony. Let them resound in an even more magnificent arrangement. Only then can the eternal symphony be manifested...

> **「心宿二」：**让它们成为交响中的一枚音符，由同谐将它们调和，融入更宏大的编排，如此那永恒乐章才能显现——

**Robin:** You know that the destiny of the cosmos never heeds the command of just one. Rather, it chases after notes that are ever more resplendent.

> **「心宿二」：**我们承认在匹诺康尼，开拓有着令人印象深刻的回响，不过那也只是「家族」应允下才存在的颤音。

**"Antares":** We don't want any unexpected mishaps. All we seek is an adequate composition, such that THEIR will may descend once more.

> **「心宿二」：**我们不希望任何意外发生，只要接下来的乐章足够圆满，祂的意志会再次降临。

**Robin:** But...

> **知更鸟：**可是……

**"Antares":** We hear the clamor in your heart, dear child. Speak your worries, and we shall offer an answer.

> **「心宿二」：**我们听到了你心中的杂音，孩子。说出你的困扰，我们来为你解答。

> **知更鸟：**我想知道…为何您不出面，而是让我去千星城？

**"Antares":** Because that place isn't Penacony's "sweet dream." As such, your singing will be more worthy of all to hear than any words spoken...

> **「心宿二」：**因为那里并非匹诺康尼的「美梦」，因此你的歌声会比任何言语，更值得被众人倾听……

**Robin:** (Are they trying to silence the clamor? Or is this...)

> **知更鸟：**（是想消除杂音么？还是……）

> **「心宿二」：**好孩子，去拨开众人眼前的阴翳吧。使他们再度直视那予取予求的伟力，敬拜三重面相降下的仁慈——

**"Antares":** The Family will be proud of you.

### Finish the endorsement shoot and speak with Ratite

> *Aventurine has also arrived in Astropolis. Apparently, he has accepted Ratite's video challenge. Of course, he has his own reasons, and that challenge is just a way to get where he really wants to be.*

[Recap] Aventurine wrapped up the commercial shoot and handed out the IPC's latest products to fans who came to see him on set. Ratite pulled Aventurine aside for a private word.

**Photographer:** Great! This expression is perfect! That's the vibe we need!

> **砂金：**大禽先生，素材还没拍够，来这里找我了？

**Ratite:** But I have a favor to ask... How about we talk somewhere private?

> **大禽：**不过，找您是有别的事…借一步说话？

[Recap] Ratite thought Aventurine was simply too rich, which made the challenge lose its entertainment value. So he asked Aventurine what he wanted to buy the most, to which Aventurine replied that it was a carefully hidden "secret."

**Ratite:** Since your challenge has already begun, in principle, I shouldn't be showing up unless absolutely necessary.

"All-You-Can-Lose-Challenge Commercial Cooperation Agreement

Party A (Challenge Participant): (Trailblazer) Party B (Program Producer & Organizer): Ratite Party B has conceived and produced an online video program called "All-You-Can-Lose Challenge" (hereinafter referred to as the "Challenge") and has invited Party A to participate as the principal guest in this episode. Having negotiated in good faith and on equal terms, Party A and Party B hereby agree as follows: Article 1: Core Challenge Terms 1.1 Challenge Goal Within 72 consecutive hours (3 calendar days) starting from the effective date of this agreement, Party A must legally complete a total consumption task of TWENTY BILLION credits within the administrative region of Astropolis. 1.2 Success and Failure (a) Success Refers to completing the consumption described in Article 1.1 within the agreed time allotment in full compliance with all restrictive clauses of this agreement. Upon success, Party A will receive a reward of ONE HUNDRED BILLION credits paid by Party B. This reward is an after-tax amount, and its nature is a permanent and irrevocable gift. (b) Failure Refers to running out of time or violating any restrictive clauses. Upon failure, Party A must, within 24 hours, reimburse Party B in full for all funds for this challenge during the challenge period, totaling TWENTY BILLION credits. Article 2: Restrictive Clauses (Consumption Rules) Party A's consumption behavior during the challenge period must strictly abide by the following rules: 2.1 Asset Retention Prohibited Consumption shall not be used to purchase any items with long-term residual value or items that can be deemed as assets, including but not limited to real estate, vehicles, precious metals, jewelry, intellectual property, corporate equity, etc. 2.2 Destruction and Gifting Prohibited Intentional destruction of purchased items is prohibited; funds shall not be used for charitable donations, public welfare undertakings, or any form of gratuitous gifting (except for remuneration paid under an employment relationship). 2.3 Consumption Subject Restrictions All consumption must be used for Party A themselves, or for labor remuneration (which must not far exceed market prices), travel, and necessary expenses provided by personnel legally hired to complete the consumption. 2.4 Legality of Behavior All consumption and conduct shall strictly comply with all applicable laws, regulations, and policies of Interastral Peace Corporation. Any involvement in pornography, gambling, narcotics, or illegal transactions is strictly prohibited. Article 3: Program Production and Revenue 3.1 Party A acknowledges and agrees that the entire process of their participation in this challenge will be recorded and produced by Party B into a video program titled "All-You-Can-Lose Challenge" for release. 3.2 All revenue generated from the program based on this cooperation, including but not limited to platform advertising revenue shares, sponsorship fees, sub-licensing revenue, etc., shall be divided equally between Party A and Party B, with each holding 50%. 3.3 Party B promises that this episode of the program will premiere on Party B's primary video platform channel no later than the seventh day after the end of the challenge period. Party B is solely responsible for the specific editing and promotion of the program. Article 4: Confidentiality Obligations 4.1 Before the public release of the program agreed upon in Article 3 of this agreement, Party A shall not disclose the existence, rules, progress, and results of this challenge to any third party in any form. 4.2 This confidentiality obligation takes effect from the date of the signing of the agreement and terminates when the program is publicly broadcast across the entire network. Article 5: Miscellaneous 5.1 During the performance of this agreement, any additional income or revenue generated by the challenge itself, including but not limited to the program revenue described in Article 3, accidentally obtained prizes, refunds, etc., must be counted into Party A's total consumption of TWENTY BILLION credits to offset it. 5.2 This agreement is made in duplicate, with Party A and Party B each holding one copy, taking effect from the date of signing by both parties. For matters not covered herein, both parties may sign a supplementary agreement separately. Party A (Signature): ____ Party B (Signature): Ratite

### Head to the nearest mall and start spending immediately

> Ratite may have underestimated you, thinking you haven't seen "B***ster's Millions" or "H***o Mr. Billionaire" (don't worry if you really haven't. Basically, the protagonists in those stories wasted way too much time not spending money). You're different. To complete the challenge successfully, you will spend money with single-minded focus. "Boss, the mall is right over there!" "I came, I spent, I conquered."

"All-You-Can-Lose Challenge" Commercial Cooperation Agreement

Party A (Challenge Participant): (Trailblazer) Party B (Program Producer & Organizer): Ratite Party B has conceived and produced an online video program called "All-You-Can-Lose Challenge" (hereinafter referred to as the "Challenge") and has invited Party A to participate as the principal guest in this episode. Having negotiated in good faith and on equal terms, Party A and Party B hereby agree as follows: Article 1: Core Challenge Terms 1.1 Challenge Goal Within 72 consecutive hours (3 calendar days) starting from the effective date of this agreement, Party A must legally complete a total consumption task of TWENTY BILLION credits within the administrative region of Astropolis. 1.2 Success and Failure (a) Success Refers to completing the consumption described in Article 1.1 within the agreed time allotment in full compliance with all restrictive clauses of this agreement. Upon success, Party A will receive a reward of ONE HUNDRED BILLION credits paid by Party B. This reward is an after-tax amount, and its nature is a permanent and irrevocable gift. (b) Failure Refers to running out of time or violating any restrictive clauses. Upon failure, Party A must, within 24 hours, reimburse Party B in full for all funds for this challenge during the challenge period, totaling TWENTY BILLION credits. Article 2: Restrictive Clauses (Consumption Rules) Party A's consumption behavior during the challenge period must strictly abide by the following rules: 2.1 Asset Retention Prohibited Consumption shall not be used to purchase any items with long-term residual value or items that can be deemed as assets, including but not limited to real estate, vehicles, precious metals, jewelry, intellectual property, corporate equity, etc. 2.2 Destruction and Gifting Prohibited Intentional destruction of purchased items is prohibited; funds shall not be used for charitable donations, public welfare undertakings, or any form of gratuitous gifting (except for remuneration paid under an employment relationship). 2.3 Consumption Subject Restrictions All consumption must be used for Party A themselves, or for labor remuneration (which must not far exceed market prices), travel, and necessary expenses provided by personnel legally hired to complete the consumption. 2.4 Legality of Behavior All consumption and conduct shall strictly comply with all applicable laws, regulations, and policies of Interastral Peace Corporation. Any involvement in pornography, gambling, narcotics, or illegal transactions is strictly prohibited. Article 3: Program Production and Revenue 3.1 Party A acknowledges and agrees that the entire process of their participation in this challenge will be recorded and produced by Party B into a video program titled "All-You-Can-Lose Challenge" for release. 3.2 All revenue generated from the program based on this cooperation, including but not limited to platform advertising revenue shares, sponsorship fees, sub-licensing revenue, etc., shall be divided equally between Party A and Party B, with each holding 50%. 3.3 Party B promises that this episode of the program will premiere on Party B's primary video platform channel no later than the seventh day after the end of the challenge period. Party B is solely responsible for the specific editing and promotion of the program. Article 4: Confidentiality Obligations 4.1 Before the public release of the program agreed upon in Article 3 of this agreement, Party A shall not disclose the existence, rules, progress, and results of this challenge to any third party in any form. 4.2 This confidentiality obligation takes effect from the date of the signing of the agreement and terminates when the program is publicly broadcast across the entire network. Article 5: Miscellaneous 5.1 During the performance of this agreement, any additional income or revenue generated by the challenge itself, including but not limited to the program revenue described in Article 3, accidentally obtained prizes, refunds, etc., must be counted into Party A's total consumption of TWENTY BILLION credits to offset it. 5.2 This agreement is made in duplicate, with Party A and Party B each holding one copy, taking effect from the date of signing by both parties. For matters not covered herein, both parties may sign a supplementary agreement separately. Party A (Signature): (Trailblazer) Party B (Signature): Ratite

**Ratite:** Ugh, I'll pay... I'll pay! I'll pay you right now! Work-Work!

> **大禽：**哎哟，我付…我付，我现在就给你们付！小勤！

**Work-Work:** I'm here.

> **小勤：**我在。

**Ratite:** Take my...

> **大禽：**把我……

**(Trailblazer):** Take your... what?

> **开拓者：**把我…的什么？

**Ratite:** Take my... my money... Waah...

> **大禽：**把我…我的钱…呜呜……

**Work-Work:** Transfer amount?

> **小勤：**多少？

**(Trailblazer):** The number of players Miss Robin defeated in the "Guess-the-Race-Winner Challenge," multiplied by 500 credits each.

> **开拓者：**「猜赛车挑战」中，知更鸟战胜的竞猜人数，乘以每人500信用点。

**Ratite:** Waaah, boo-hoo-hoo...!

> **大禽：**呜呜呜，呜呜…！

**Work-Work:** Please enunciate!

> **小勤：**好好说话！

**Ratite:** ...Transfer it all... waaah... to Miss Robin.

> **大禽：**…全部…呜呜…转给知更鸟小姐。

**Work-Work:** Understood. Payment successful √

> **小勤：**好嘞，支付成功√

**Robin:** Thank you, Work-Work. I've received it.

> **知更鸟：**谢谢你小勤，我收到了哦。

**Ratite:** Waaaaaaaah........!!!

> **大禽：**呜————

**Work-Work:** Is that... the Astral Express blowing its whistle?

> **小勤：**这是…星穹列车发车的声音？

**(Trailblazer):** That's the sound of Ratite achieving liftoff.

> **开拓者：**这是大禽起飞的声音。

**Aventurine:** We can't let him off the hook just yet. We still have our own score to settle with him. Any ideas, (Trailblazer)?

> **砂金：**还不能就这样便宜了大禽。我们和他那笔账还没算呢，开拓者你有什么想法吗？

- ▸ ...Edit videos.

> ▸ …剪片子。

- ▸ ...The footage review room.

> ▸ …审片室。

**(Trailblazer):** How's about... we lock Ratite in "the room you can't leave until you finish editing," and make him edit this entire episode of the "Guess-the-Race-Winner Challenge" himself.

> **开拓者：**不如这样…把大禽关进「不剪完片子就不许离开的房间」，让他把这期「猜赛车挑战」剪完。

**(Trailblazer):** Consider it his way of leaving us all with a beautiful holiday memory.

> **开拓者：**也算是为大家留下一份美好的假期回忆吧。

**Aventurine:** Brilliant idea. I was thinking the exact same thing. Let's do it.

> **砂金：**好主意，想到一块儿去了。就这么办！

## 068　The Challenge Ends, and Now...　挑战结束，然后…

> 世界：Astropolis　|　区域：Astropolis Central District　|　角色：Trailblazer; Robin • Summeretto; Siobhan; Aventurine • Waveflair; Sugilite; Ratite　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''The day of Guess-the-Race-Winner Challenge'' completed

### Talk to Siobhan

> *The Lost Echo of Gopher Wood is, at its heart, an invitation addressed to Robin. Yet only the little bird herself knows the way forward.*

[Recap] After witnessing Robin's clash with The Family, Siobhan, acting as an intermediary, extended an invitation for Robin to join the Blues Brigade, hoping to pursue an alternative vision of Harmony together. Robin declined, saying the Brigade had yet to earn her seal of approval. She'd rather keep her own destiny in hand and give herself the freedom to shape her own future.

Some time later, somewhere in Astropolis...

**Robin:** I wondered how you'd keep tabs on me, and how you'd judge my actions.

> **知更鸟：**…我想过，你们要如何了解我的动向，又从何评价我的行为。

**Robin:** And then I knew. It's actually quite simple: Just assign an anonymous, independent evaluator to shadow me.

> **知更鸟：**后来我意识到，答案其实很简单——只需要安排一位匿名的独立评审员，一直跟随在我身边就好。

**Robin:** So, Miss Siobhan, is this the real reason you came to work as a temporary assistant? To see how I'd handle your gift... or rather, your test?

> **知更鸟：**所以，舒翁小姐，这就是你这次来做临时「助理」的真正原因，来看看我会怎么应对你们的礼物…或者说，测试？

**Robin:** Or have you been watching me since long before that?

> **知更鸟：**又或者…其实你们的观察，从更早之前就已经开始了？

**Siobhan:** Don't get me wrong, I'm just a middlewoman. Besides, I was perfectly willing to share your burdens. I'd say I was a fairly dedicated assistant, wouldn't you agree?

> **舒翁：**别误会，我只是个中间人。更何况，我本就愿意为你分忧，作为助理也还算尽职尽责，对吧？

**Robin:** If I had known, I would've asked you to reject The Family on my behalf. But I suppose this was also part of your assessment?

> **知更鸟：**早知道就该让你去回绝家族的…不过我想，这也算是你们评价的一部分？

**Siobhan:** Yes. The Family might sweep this under the rug publicly, but internally, everyone is going to know exactly where you stand from now on.

> **舒翁：**是啊，即便「家族」不会公开表态，但从今往后，你的态度在「家族」内也会人尽皆知。

**Robin:** That's fine. It's something I'll have to face sooner or later anyway.

> **知更鸟：**没关系，这些我迟早要面对。

**Siobhan:** You accepted our gift and broke free from The Family's chains, following your own free will. You answered us with your song, a true "improvisation."

> **舒翁：**你收下了我们的礼物，凭自身意志挣脱了「家族」的桎梏，这份歌声就是你给出的答案——它是一次真正的「即兴演奏」。

**Robin:** To me, singing should be about "setting the tone," rather than a simple "tuning."

> **知更鸟：**对我而言，歌声应当是一场「定调」，而非一次简单的「调律」。

**Siobhan:** And that's exactly what we're looking for: another possibility for the Harmony.

> **舒翁：**这也是我们所追寻的，关于「同谐」的另一种可能。

**Siobhan:** Miss Robin, congratulations on passing the initiation test. We invite you to join the "Blues Brigade."

> **舒翁：**知更鸟小姐，恭喜你通过入会测试——我们正式邀请你，加入「蓝调旅团」。

**Siobhan:** Are you willing to inherit Mr. Gopher Wood's title of "CHORUS" and become a member of the "Blues Brigade"?

> **舒翁：**你是否愿意继承歌斐木先生的「CHORUS」之名，成为「蓝调旅团」的一员？

**Siobhan:** The Brigade wants to hear a variety of voices, not a monotonous chant. We would never restrict our members' "freedom."

> **舒翁：**旅团愿意聆听丰富的声音，而非单调的旋律——我们从不限制成员的「自由」。

**Robin:** I'm honored to be recognized by the Brigade...

> **知更鸟：**很荣幸能得到旅团的认可……

**Robin:** But the Brigade hasn't earned my recognition yet.

> **知更鸟：**但是，旅团还没得到我的认可。

**Robin:** I don't see this as a one-way gift, but a two-way choice.

> **知更鸟：**我不认为这是单向的馈赠，而应是双向的选择。

**Siobhan:** ...Oh?

> **舒翁：**…哦？

**Robin:** Mr. Gopher Wood might have left The Family, but he never walked the true path of Harmony, either.

> **知更鸟：**歌斐木先生虽然离开了「家族」，却并未真正走上「同谐」的道路。

**Robin:** If the Brigade simply provides shelter for those who reject The Family, then I'm afraid our visions for the future don't quite align.

> **知更鸟：**如果旅团仅仅是一个收留不顺从「家族」之人的组织，恐怕与我对未来的期许并不相同。

**Robin:** Going through all of this has made me realize something. The Family's blueprint, your invitation... none of that really matters.

> **知更鸟：**在这次经历后，我发现重要的既不是「家族」的蓝图，也不是你们的邀请。

**Robin:** What helped me find my rhythm again, was that carefree sea breeze and those syncopated notes.

> **知更鸟：**反倒是那一阵随性的海风，与踩在反拍上的音符，让我重新找回对掌握节奏的感知。

**Siobhan:** Carefree sea breeze, huh.

> **舒翁：**呵呵，随性的海风么……

**Siobhan:** Your sponsor, "SWING," was right. You aren't easily swayed.

> **舒翁：**果然就像你的引荐人「SWING」所言，你不会轻易被说服。

**Siobhan:** Your heart will always beat for the people, and your wings are destined to soar even beyond these skies.

> **舒翁：**你的心始终会为万人鼓动，你的羽翼注定丈量比天空更远的地方……

**Siobhan:** Maybe this is what "freedom" is supposed to be.

> **舒翁：**或许，这就是「自由」该有的样子吧。

### Call Sugilite

> *After a brief clash with Sugilite, Aventurine has made some headway in uncovering the traitor among the Ten Stonehearts. Time to pull off that mask.*

[Recap] Sugilite considered Aventurine to have barely proven himself this time, but would continue to scrutinize his colleagues, Aventurine included, as he filtered candidates for Diamond's Ten Stonehearts. With one suspect ruled out, Sugilite advised Aventurine to keep a close eye on two colleagues whose paths were soon to cross with the Astral Express: Obsidian, and Opal.

Meanwhile, somewhere in Astropolis...

**Aventurine:** Leaving your seat before the show's over? I guess this little game in Astropolis wasn't enough to hold your attention, Sugilite.

> **砂金：**戏还没散场就先离席，看来千星城的这局游戏没能入你的眼啊，舒俱。

**Aventurine:** During the disciplinary vote to strip me of my seat among the Ten Stonehearts, you were the first to vote in favor.

> **砂金：**当初在剥夺我「石心十人」位列资格的处罚决议上，你可是第一个投下「赞成」票的。

**Sugilite:** That's far from the only resolution I've voted "yes" on. How else would I get to see the looks on everyone's faces?

> **舒俱：**我投过赞成票的决议，远不止这一场。否则，我怎么能亲眼看到其他人对此的表情？

**Sugilite:** Besides, my vote was far from unjust. You never took the Cornerstone seriously to begin with.

> **舒俱：**再说，这一票不冤吧？你压根就没把基石放在眼里。

**Aventurine:** Who would have guessed that Penacony's "Dreammaster" would entrust the secrets of the Harmony to one of the Ten Stonehearts? Had I known you two were so chummy, I wouldn't have had to smash a Cornerstone over it.

> **砂金：**谁又能想到，匹诺康尼的「梦主」竟会将同谐的秘密托付给一位石心十人的同僚，如果早知道你们交情至此，我也不必为此碎掉一枚基石了。

**Sugilite:** Are you that upset you couldn't squeeze what Diamond wanted out of me?

> **舒俱：**没从我这拿到钻石想要的东西，让你这么失望吗？

**Aventurine:** Crossing off an option isn't a waste of time. What about you, though? You didn't get to see me crash and burn, yet again. Aren't you disappointed?

> **砂金：**排除一个选项，也不算浪费时间。倒是你，又一次没能如愿看到我的失败，不觉得遗憾吗？

**Sugilite:** I knew we weren't cut from the same cloth the first time I laid eyes on you. I've seen way too many upstarts like you in the Strategic Investment Department, climbing the ladder on sheer luck.

> **舒俱：**从见你第一面我就知道，我们不是一路人。像你这样凭着运气爬上来的暴发户，我在战略投资部见过太多了。

**Sugilite:** You barely managed to prove yourself this time. But if the day comes when you show your true colors like the rest of them...

> **舒俱：**这次…你勉强证明了自己。但如果有一天，你和那些人一样露出本性……

**Sugilite:** The scales will tip, the sword will fall, and you won't be nearly as lucky.

> **舒俱：**天平会倾斜，悬剑会落下，你不会同这次一样幸运。

**Aventurine:** And there's something I could never figure out. When it comes to wealth, hardly anyone in the Ten Stonehearts holds a candle to you, yet you've been sitting at the bottom rank all these years. Now I get it.

> **砂金：**有件事我一直想不通，论财富，「石心十人」中几乎无人能同你相比，但你却常年据守末位。现在我明白了——

**Aventurine:** This way, anyone trying to join the ranks of the Stonehearts has to step over the threshold known as "Sugilite" and face your scrutiny.

> **砂金：**这意味着所有跻身「石心」之人，都必须要跨过名为「舒俱」的门槛，经受你最直接的审视。

**Aventurine:** For Diamond, you're filtering people out, testing those given the name of a "Stoneheart" to see if they can bear the weight of a Cornerstone. And for yourself, wealth, status... you get whatever you want.

> **砂金：**对钻石来说，你是在替他筛选，得到「石心」之名的人，是否能承受「基石」之重。而对你来说，财富，地位，你要什么就有什么。

**Aventurine:** Life doesn't thrill you anymore. Watching the tragic downfall of others is the only way to fill the empty heart of a Mourning Actor.

> **砂金：**生命对你而言已毫无波澜，唯有观赏他人的不幸坠落，才能填补那颗属于悲悼伶人的空虚的心。

**Aventurine:** I'm still happy about the choice I made years ago. A sip of the Elation's fine wine every now and then is great. But even someone like you, aren't you still wearing a mask, playing whatever part Diamond hands you?

> **砂金：**我依然庆幸当年的选择。偶尔尝尝「欢愉」的美酒是不错，可即便如你，不也依然戴着面具，扮演钻石分派给你的角色么？

**Sugilite:** These days, even the Tavern doesn't welcome Actors anymore. What better stage to sit back and watch a tragedy unfold than the IPC?

> **舒俱：**如今连酒馆都不再欢迎伶人了。还有哪里比公司更适合欣赏悲剧的舞台呢？

**Sugilite:** If you ask me, this show is far from over. Why don't we check in on our other colleagues? Two more of the Ten Stonehearts are about to pay your old friend a visit.

> **舒俱：**要我说，这场戏码远未结束，不如看看剩下的同事吧。又有两位石心十人，要找上你的老朋友了——

**Aventurine:** I had no idea. Who will it be this time?

> **砂金：**我都不知道。这次是谁？

**Sugilite:** Obsidian and Opal.

> **舒俱：**龙晶，还有欧泊。

**Aventurine:** ...Why send them?

> **砂金：**…为什么要出动他们？

**Sugilite:** Don't ask questions you already know the answers to. If there's a good cop, there has to be a bad cop too.

> **舒俱：**知道答案的问题，就别问出口了。有人唱红脸，就有人唱白脸。

**Sugilite:** You know exactly how those two operate. They either get what they want, or they burn it to the ground.

> **舒俱：**这两人的作风你心知肚明，要么得到，要么毁掉。

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Sugilite:** I can't wait to see how you choose, Aventurine.

> **舒俱：**你会怎么选，我拭目以待，砂金。

### Head to the pit garage and speak with Robin and Aventurine

> *You were suspicious of this whole "free lunch" deal from the start. And sure enough, it turned out to be far from simple... Fortunately, all those twists and turns ended up paying off with a seriously generous reward. The vacation's not over yet. Once everyone's tied up their loose ends, you three can finally hang out and actually enjoy a good holiday. More importantly, you've made a small fortune. So... what's the plan? Don't worry, there are no nasty traps this time. You already know exactly what you want...*

**(Trailblazer):** (I'm supposed to meet Aventurine and Robin at the team's garage. They should be there by now, right?)

> **开拓者：**（和砂金、知更鸟约在车队的P房里见面，他们应该到了吧？）

*(Approach Robin)*

[Recap] After settling her debts, Robin donated all her surplus earnings to the Foundation, while you took out an astronomically expensive insurance policy on the ever-calamity-prone Astral Express. Robin gifted you a record of the new song the two of you created together, and Aventurine extended an invitation to keep an eye out for news on Starflux's upcoming grand prix. Meanwhile, Ratite was holed up in the footage review room, buried deep in an endless editing session with no end in sight.

**Robin:** Thanks to Mr. Aventurine pulling some strings, the paperwork for the prize money is already sorted.

> **知更鸟：**多亏砂金先生打点，奖金的财务流程已经走完了。

**Robin:** The Foundation called me three times to triple-check if I was fully awake when I wrote down that number...

> **知更鸟：**基金会特地打来三通电话，反复向我确认数字是否是我本人在清醒时填写的……

**Aventurine:** Understandable, though. Even after clearing the debt, there's a pretty hefty amount left. And Miss Robin donated every last credit to the Foundation.

> **砂金：**这也难怪。毕竟在偿还债务之后，盈余依然相当可观，更何况知更鸟小姐还把这笔钱都捐给了基金会。

**Robin:** I could not have done it without your immense support.

> **知更鸟：**也少不了你们两位的大力支持。

**Aventurine:** It's the least we could do. (Trailblazer), what are you going to do with the rest of your cut?

> **砂金：**一点心意罢了。开拓者，你剩下的那部分打算怎么处理？

- ▸ Actually, I've had a bit of a revelation lately.

**(Trailblazer):** The secret to "happy spending" is actually pretty simple: It's all about fulfilling your true needs.

> **开拓者：**「快乐花钱」的原理其实很简单——就是满足自己的真实需求。

**(Trailblazer):** If treating yourself turns into a task, it becomes joyless and burdensome.

> **开拓者：**如果这份满足变成了交差或任务，心里就只剩下负担，快乐也会打折扣。

**(Trailblazer):** So this time, I'm going to be a little "selfish." I'll spend it if I feel like it, and I won't if I don't.

> **开拓者：**所以，这一次我要「自私」一点——想花就花，不想花就不花。

**Robin:** Even minus the debt, you've still got tens of billions in hand... Sounds like you've got a plan though?

> **知更鸟：**去掉债务后，到手也有上百亿了吧…看样子你已经想好了？

- ▸ I bought vehicle insurance for the Astral Express.

**(Trailblazer):** I don't know when it started, but the Astral Express keeps ending up in the craziest of situations...

> **开拓者：**不知道从什么时候开始，星穹列车就总是陷入各种极端状况……

**(Trailblazer):** Getting blown up, getting rammed, cars detaching left and right, at times turning into the horizon's echo... you name it.

> **开拓者：**被炸毁、被撞击、时而车厢脱节，时而化身为地平线的长音…等等。

**(Trailblazer):** So my top priority is getting the Astral Express covered.

> **开拓者：**所以当务之急，就是给星穹列车上个保险。

**Robin:** I'm no expert, but with a track record like that, would any insurance company take the policy?

> **知更鸟：**虽然我不是专业人士，但这种情况…真会有保险公司愿意接单吗？

**Robin:** Wouldn't they be handing you free money?

> **知更鸟：**这不就等于给你们送钱吗？

**(Trailblazer):** I paid an astronomical premium for the policy.

> **开拓者：**我也交了天价保费。

**Aventurine:** Ah, so that client is you.

> **砂金：**原来那个人是你啊。

**Aventurine:** Sugilite told me that insurance company of his that's always in the red just took on another guaranteed-loss policy, purely because he thought it'd be amusing.

> **砂金：**我听舒俱说了，他名下那家常年亏损的保险公司，最近又接了一笔注定赔钱的单子，纯粹因为他觉得有趣。

**Aventurine:** Watch your back. Don't let him put a target on you.

> **砂金：**你也当心点，别被他盯上。

**Robin:** Oh, by the way, (Trailblazer), the new single is officially released. It's our first collaboration, so please enjoy~

> **知更鸟：**对了，开拓者，这次新歌已经正式发布了。这是你和我合作的第一首单曲，敬请惠存～

- ▸ Thanks! Loved working with you~
- ▸ Putting it on the jukebox right now!
- ▸ Next stop, karaoke!

**Robin:** One more thing. The bets for the "Guess-the-Race-Winner Challenge" were honored, but the Starflux Grand Prix results won't count toward the main championship points. It was classified as a "fantasy exhibition race."

> **知更鸟：**还有一件事——尽管「猜赛车挑战」的胜负得到了承认，但「星速引力」的比赛结果并不会计入正赛积分，它被定性为一场「梦幻的表演赛」。

- ▸ It did go way beyond normal car racing!
- ▸ Fantasy is an understatement. It was pure magic.

**Aventurine:** The organizers are going to announce a new grand prix soon. Since you've got a racing team on your hands, you might want to keep an eye out.

> **砂金：**最近主办方会发布新杯赛的消息，你手下还有一支车队，不妨也关注一下。

- ▸ Will do!

**(Trailblazer):** Look at the new video Ratite just dropped.

> **开拓者：**你们看，大禽新上传的视频……

**Aventurine:** "After Losing 100 Billion Credits, I Did These 3 Things Right..."

> **砂金：**《在输掉了千亿信用点之后，我做对了这三件事情…》

**Ratite:** After I lost 100 billion credits, everyone thought I was finished.

> **大禽：**在输掉了千亿信用点之后，所有人都以为我完了。

**Ratite:** All two thousand contacts in my phone turned into strangers overnight.

> **大禽：**通讯录里两千个联系人，一夜之间全部变成了「陌生人」。

**Ratite:** Stuck in that pitch-black footage review room, I didn't beg, and I didn't complain. Instead, I did three things no one saw coming.

> **大禽：**在暗无天日的审片室里，我没有求人，没有抱怨，而是做了三件所有人都没想到的事。

**Ratite:** And today, I'm sharing these crucial life lessons with you, zero reservations!

> **大禽：**——今天，我就把这些最重要的经验，毫无保留地分享给你！

**Ratite:** Tap to unlock premium content and get my step-by-step tutorial! The price of a cup of coffee, for a chance to turn your life around!

> **大禽：**点击解锁付费内容，学习我的实操细节。一杯咖啡钱，换一个改变人生的机会……

- ▸ Begone, you big bird!
- ▸ Ratite's lost his mind!
